#  DraftKings King of the Court: Favorites and Sleeper Picks (11/17/26) 
[ See RotoBaller at the top of Google ](https://google.com/preferences/source?q=rotoballer.com) by [Koby Hanson](https://www.rotoballer.com/author/singedpancakes "Posts by Koby Hanson")
Link copied to clipboard!
Reddit Twitter Facebook Copy link
[ @RotoBaller ](https://twitter.com/RotoBaller)
![Scottie Barnes - Fantasy Basketball Rankings, NBA Injury News, DFS Lineup Picks](https://www.rotoballer.com/wp-content/uploads/2022/10/Scottie-Barnes-Fantasy-Basketball-Rankings-NBA-Injury-News-DFS-Lineup-Picks-icon-rotoballer-1220x815.jpeg) Nov 17, 2026, 7:12 AM ET
Koby Hanson provides his favorite players to target with prop bets tonight (Tuesday, 11/17) in DraftKings King of the Court contest. Turn five bucks into a big payout by finding the right player to bet on every week this season!
Another King of the Court contest is upon us! We will, however, have to navigate two of the chalkiest plays in the NBA on this small five-game slate. [Franz Wagner](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002654/Franz+Wagner) won last week, but [Shai Gilgeous-Alexander](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001999/Shai+Gilgeous-Alexander) wasn't far behind, and if [Damian Lillard](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000619/Damian+Lillard) and the Blazers hadn't had an explosive 3rd quarter that kept SGA out of playing the 4th, I think we'd have nailed SGA last week.
With plenty of options, I'll narrow it down to five spots, including two of the more popular options on the slate, plus a few pivots I'm considering. If you are looking for any other bets or DFS help, make sure to check out the RotoBaller Premium Discord where I and other writers will be in there chatting about the upcoming slate and any updates that might pop up along the way!
In this article, I will provide my favorite picks for the **DraftKings King of the Court contest on** **Tuesday, November 17th.** And don't forget to grab a [RotoBaller Premium Pass](https://www.rotoballer.com/all-premium-access-nfl-mlb-nba-nhl), which is highly discounted and includes DFS, betting, props, and season-long tools like the Team Sync platform. 
_**Featured Promo:** New Novig users get a $25 purchase match (50% discount up to $25) on your first Novig deposit, and [6 free months of RotoBaller's "Big-4" Premium Pass](https://www.rotoballer.com/no) (NBA, NFL, MLB, NHL) which includes exclusive tools for Betting, Props, DFS and more!_ [CLAIM IT NOW](https://www.rotoballer.com/no)
## How King of the Court Works
King of the Court has quickly become very popular amongst DraftKings users. It involves picking who you believe will be the player with the highest PRA (Points/Rebounds/Assists) total for the night. You don't even have to win the prop bet to win the KOTC! You simply have to make a qualifying wager of $5 or more on the player who finishes as the leader in PRA.
There are a few ways to go about it; a lot of people will take the chalk and take the small bonus bets they get with it (since you have to split the pot with hundreds of thousands of other bettors). Or if you are like me, you like to get a little different, and that can lead to some big payouts!
I hit a few of these last season, including the game where [Bam Adebayo](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001836/Bam+Adebayo) went for 83 points! That led to me landing over 1k in bonus bets!
[![](data:image/svg+xml,<svg%20xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"%20viewBox="0%200%20600%20630"></svg>)](https://www.rotoballer.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/KOTC-e1792576683574.png)
## DraftKings King of the Court Picks: The Chalk
_All odds are current as of 7:00 AM and courtesy of DraftKings Sportsbook._
**[Victor Wembanyama](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003031/Victor+Wembanyama) over 27.5 Points (-122)**
One of the most popular choices on this slate will clearly be Victor Wembanyama. I actually prefer Wemby compared to [Luka Doncic](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001984/Luka+Don%C4%8Di%C4%87) tonight, if we are looking at matchup only.
Big men have had quite a bit of success against the Pistons. We have seen [Alexandre Sarr](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003215/Alexandre+Sarr), [Karl-Anthony Towns](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001431/Karl-Anthony+Towns), and [Joel Embiid](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000726/Joel+Embiid) all finish with 40+ PRA against them already this season. Wemby is averaging 31/12/3.7 over the last 10 games. He also has a 35% Usage rate, which is good for the third-best in the league, and is averaging an absurd 1.46 PRA per minute this season.
Wemby doesn't need a lot of minutes to finish with a ton of stats. Earlier this season, he had 54 PRA in just 25 minutes.
He has had multiple games this season with over 2.0 PRA per minute. With a game that should be relatively close, if Wemby gets over 30 minutes, we could see him at well over 60 PRA.
_Note: This was written before[Austin Reaves](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002720/Austin+Reaves) and [Walker Kessler](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002889/Walker+Kessler) were ruled out, which makes Luka much more viable. The spread dropped from 15 points to just 7.5, and he'll surely be a popular option. If you're betting on him tonight, his safest prop is probably over 36.5 points (-110). He's averaging 39 per game on a massive 40% usage rate._
## DraftKings King of the Court Picks: The Pivots
_All odds are current as of 7:00 AM and courtesy of DraftKings Sportsbook._
When you are going after players such as Luka or Wemby, you definitely have a higher probability of hitting, but everyone else is taking them, too. It makes it likely that the payout will be quite minimal. So having other options in great spots, and hoping they pop off while guys like Luka maybe have a bit of a low-key game, is ideal when trying to go for as big of bonus bets as possible!
Wemby and Luka will be the top guys on the slate, but let's take a look at a few guys who could give them a run for their money.
**[Cade Cunningham](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002667/Cade+Cunningham) over 38.5 Points, Rebounds, and Assists (-109)**
Keeping things here with the Pistons/Spurs game. Cade Cunningham has been crushing it over the last week; he is coming off a double-overtime win that had him finish with 52 points, five rebounds, and 11 assists. He is also coming off a 33-point performance against the Heat on Saturday. He is now coming into this one against the Spurs on two days of rest, and with a top-10 usage rate this year at just over 30%.
The Spurs have been relatively solid on defense against the PG position, but continue to give up over 25 PPG and nearly eight assists. Over the last 10 games, Cunningham has a 1.15 PRA per minute, and with this game expected to be close, he should play 36+ minutes. He is averaging 63 passes a game, and if the rest of the team can sink their shots, he should see a double-double pretty easily.
**[Deni Avdija](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002534/Deni+Avdija) over 24.5 Points (-105)**
Back-to-backs against the same team are generally situations to avoid. Teams adjust their defenses to what was wrong the day before, and the big thing for Minnesota was not being able to stop Deni Avdija. I do expect them to put more pressure on Deni, but generally it leads to him getting to the line more. He is already in the top five in the NBA in free throws this year, and is coming off a 27-point performance last night against the T-Wolves.
He had 24 of those points in the first half; the Blazers started to lose control of the game, and eventually Deni played fewer minutes than he normally would. He still finished with 39 PRA, while playing roughly 5-6 minutes less than he would. Deni is 11th in the league in USG%, with 29.3%. The offense generally runs through him, and he has been shooting the three ball quite a bit more, too.
On a smaller slate like this, Deni will garner some decent ownership, but I'm hoping with Luka and Wemby on the slate we can dodge some of that and still end up with a sizeable payout. I have Deni projected in the 45-48 PRA range, which could give Wemby a run for his money if he has a down game.
**[Scottie Barnes](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002662/Scottie+Barnes) over 34.5 Points, Rebounds, and Assists (+103)**
It didn't take long for Scottie Barnes to get back into the groove; he smoked the team he is playing tonight for 30 points on Sunday. He took 27 shots, which is a little more than his average, but in a close game like this, I can see it being necessary. The Raptors have been playing a slightly tighter rotation lately, and Scottie Barnes should continue seeing 35+ minutes.
His PRA is fairly low for his production this season; especially without [Kawhi Leonard](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000646/Kawhi+Leonard) in the lineup, Barnes takes on a bigger role. He has soared over this line in six of his last nine games. Without Leonard in the lineup, Barnes has a 33% USG rate this season. This game should be one of the closest on the slate, and if any game has potential to go to OT, it could be this one. I also see this game getting a bit overlooked in terms of KOTC, so it might end up being a great pivot if you are looking to be different than the field.
**[Keyonte George](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003044/Keyonte+George) over 6.5 Assists (+109)**
This one might be seen as a bit of a long shot, but I really like Keyonte George now that he is the main focus of the offense. With [Lauri Markkanen](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001831/Lauri+Markkanen) and [Jaren Jackson Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001991/Jaren+Jackson+Jr.) out, that really only leaves Keyonte George and [Darryn Peterson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003708/Darryn+Peterson) to do most of the scoring. In the five games, with those two out, George is also averaging just over 11 RA a game, too. We also saw George have a near 40-point explosion against the Pelicans.
This game should be one of the higher-scoring games on the slate, and both teams should be able to keep each other close. The Jazz were keeping up with the Spurs just the other night for most of the game. This Jazz team can be scrappy, and against a Clippers defense that is allowing the third most points and averaging just over 13 RA a game to opposing PGs, this is lining up to be a great spot.
George leads the team in USG% at 31% without Markkanen and JJJ in the offense. As long as the game stays close, I like George as a long shot to make us some big bonus bets.

#  NBA Best Bets: Player Prop Picks (Monday, 11/16/26)

NBA player prop bets and statistical analysis for Monday, November 16. Matchup advantages, projection edges, and value lines for tonight's slate.
The NFL had its time in the spotlight yesterday, but now that the NBA season is in full swing, basketball owns Mondays once again. No one cares about the Chargers or the Ravens (outside of Baltimore), right? There's one crappy NFL game tonight, but a pretty sweet 8-game NBA slate without a single double-digit spread (no blowouts tonight, right?)
For today's picks, I'll include the best odds available at one of the books I use, but I'll also include the price at [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), which is one of our partners and absolutely one of the best sports prediction markets out there.

## NBA Player Prop Bets
**[Fred VanVleet](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001733/Fred+VanVleet) OVER 13.5 Points (+100 FanDuel)**
_Compare at Novig at -102_
The Rockets didn't even need their veteran point guard last night to dispatch the Wizards with ease. Since he sat out of the first half of a two-game set, FVV is expected back in the lineup and tonight and should be well-rested for his matchup with a surging Heat team.
VanVleet has been surging himself, considering that he's scored at least 16 points in four of his last five games and six of his last eight. We're seeing him log around 30 minutes now pretty consistently, and he's really improved his efficiency this season compared to others. He's taking fewer shots, but better shots, as his role is really to help run the offense, play good defense, and provide veteran leadership.
But FVV can still get buckets and knock down open looks. He has a solid matchup here tonight as the Heat have allowed the most points in the NBA to opposing point guards this season. Miami is playing well and should be able to hold their own here, which means a high likelihood of a high-scoring close game where our player prop targets see their full allotment of minutes.
**[Cam Spencer](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003254/Cam+Spencer) OVER 7.5 Assists (-104 Novig)**
Spencer is going to draw another start for the Grizzlies tonight as [Ty Jerome](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002307/Ty+Jerome) has already been ruled out. Memphis has also been without another guard, [Scotty Pippen Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002915/Scotty+Pippen+Jr.), now for nearly two weeks. That leaves Cam running the offense tonight, with Jevon Small likely backing him up.
Spencer was fantastic on Saturday while filling in for Jerome, racking up eight assists in a win over Brooklyn. His only other start this season was back on Halloween, when he dropped 10 points and 13 assists on the Kings in just 25 minutes.
Washington is allowing the second-most assists to opposing point guards this season. Plus, they are in the second half of a back-to-back tonight after getting drubbed by the Rockets last night.
This game has a healthy 232.5 total, and Memphis is only a 3.5-point favorite. We need the Wizards to keep it close, but I think they will, especially with the Grizzlies a little undermanned due to a few other injuries.
**[Yaxel Lendeborg](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003717/Yaxel+Lendeborg) OVER 22.5 Points (-108 Novig)**
The Warriors were embarrassed by the Spurs the other night, and their rookie standout Lendeborg was very vocal about how hard he took it. With [Stephen Curry](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000485/Stephen+Curry) out for the next month, this is going to be Yaxel's team as far as being the lead scorer, and he couldn't ask for a better matchup tonight as the Pelicans come into this one in the midst of trade talks, turmoil, and six straight losses.
They finally stopped the bleeding on defense a bit on Saturday, limiting the Nuggets to 116 points. But they had allowed 138 or more points in four straight games before that. Getting [Yves Missi](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003206/Yves+Missi) back and playing [Herbert Jones](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002699/Herbert+Jones) more minutes will help, but I still don't see the Pelicans stopping the Warriors or Lendeborg tonight.
The first time these teams met, Yaxel had 23 points in only 27 minutes, and Stephen Curry had 25 points in 20 minutes. Without Curry in the lineup, expect Lendeborg to take close to 20 shots tonight. I think he can clear this bar relatively easily, and we've already seen him go for 25 or more five times this year.

## NBA Value Player Props
**[Day'Ron Sharpe](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002697/Day'Ron+Sharpe) OVER 3.5 Assists (-108 Novig)**
The Nets have a very non-traditional offense in that they don't have a pass-first point guard on their roster or in their starting lineup. The offense runs through [Julius Randle](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000710/Julius+Randle) and Day'Ron Sharpe at the power forward and center positions. Both players are averaging close to five assists per game to lead the team, but it's been Sharpe who has been the most reliable passer lately.
The big man has four or more assists in five straight games and six of his last seven. Over this most recent five-game stretch, he's averaging over seven assists per game and registered a 13-assist game against the Hawks as part of a poor man's triple-double (12-10-13).
The only concern here is foul trouble, as he's definitely found his way into it this year and will have to spend some time covering [Joel Embiid](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000726/Joel+Embiid). I think he has the potential to play 25+ minutes tonight in what should be a pretty competitive game, and four assists could be a breeze with [Egor Demin](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003455/Egor+Demin) back in the lineup as another perimeter shooter who can knock down shots when fed by Sharpe's passes.
**[Alexandre Sarr](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003215/Alexandre+Sarr) OVER 6.5 Rebounds (-125 Novig)**
Hey, we had this prop last week, too, in Wednesday's article. And guess what he did in that game? He easily went over with eight boards in just 25 minutes against the Pacers.
Since that game, Sarr has had two masterpieces in a row, including a really solid effort last night against the Rockets, even in a game they lost by 28 points.
That makes eight games in a row with at least eight boards. We just need seven tonight! It's pretty clear that [Anthony Davis](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000468/Anthony+Davis) doesn't mind deferring to Sarr on the boards so far this year, so look for him to continue to stack up rebounds while he's out there.
**[Cedric Coward](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003477/Cedric+Coward) OVER 10.5 Rebounds + Assists (-120 FanDuel)**
_Compare at Novig at -122_
If ain't broke, then don't fix it! If you read this column last week, you'll remember that I have been on this prop for Coward for a while. He was at 9.5 last week, but his continued solid play has pushed this number up to 10.5 now.
However, Coward is still crushing the over on 10.5 over his last three games, logging rebound and assist totals of 15, 13, and 16. He has 9+ in every single game this season, and continues to be both a force on the boards and a secondary shot-creator in this offense.
With Ty Jerome out tonight, look for Coward to help initiate the offense along with Cam Spencer and Cam Boozer. We saw him play 33 minutes in Saturday's game against Brooklyn, and I think we'll see another 30+ minutes for him tonight as the Grizzlies need to lean on him a bit more with Jerome and others out.
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!

#  NBA Best Bets: Player Prop Bet Picks (Friday, 11/13/26) 
[ See RotoBaller at the top of Google ](https://google.com/preferences/source?q=rotoballer.com) by [Keith Eyster](https://www.rotoballer.com/author/eys819 "Posts by Keith Eyster")

NBA player prop bets and statistical analysis for Friday, November 13. Matchup advantages, projection edges, and value lines for tonight's slate.
We're back for another Friday slate of NBA Cup action! There are nine games on the schedule, highlighted by an Eastern Conference showdown between the Cleveland Cavaliers and the Miami Heat.
For today's picks, I'll include the best odds available at one of the books I use, but I'll also include the price at [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), which is one of our partners and absolutely one of the best sports prediction markets out there.
As always, follow [RotoBaller on X](https://x.com/RotoBaller) and download the [RotoBaller app](https://www.rotoballer.com/app) for all your fantasy football and betting needs. Now, here are some of my favorite NBA bets and player props for Friday's NBA slate!
_**Featured Promo:** New Novig users get a $25 purchase match (50% discount up to $25) on your first Novig deposit, and [6 free months of RotoBaller's "Big-4" Premium Pass](https://www.rotoballer.com/no) (NFL, NBA, MLB, NHL) which includes exclusive tools for Betting, Props, DFS and more!_ [CLAIM IT NOW](https://www.rotoballer.com/no)
## NBA Player Prop Bets
We have a bit of a theme today (not planned, I promise) with a lot of big men popping in great rebounding spots!
**[Karl-Anthony Towns](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001431/Karl-Anthony+Towns) OVER 33.5 Points + Rebounds (-122 Novig)**
Towns is averaging 20.9 points and 13.5 rebounds this season. He's cleared this points-and-rebounds line in two of his last three games, and now he gets an elite matchup against Indiana.
The Pacers have played at the sixth-fastest pace in the league this season, and they rank 14th in defensive efficiency. They have also allowed the fourth-most rebounds in the league.
The Knicks have already dropped one game in NBA Cup play, so they should be highly motivated to win here. That could mean upper-30s minutes for Towns, which would set him up well to clear this prop.
**[Darius Acuff Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003712/Darius+Acuff+Jr.) OVER 24.5 Points (-108 Novig)**
Acuff is averaging 26.2 points per game, and he's cleared this line in six of 11 games. The most exciting part is that he is playing huge minutes no matter the game script. He just played 35 minutes in a 25-point loss to the Clippers, and he played over 36 minutes in the game before against Portland.
He gets that same Portland matchup today, and he went for 33 points in their prior meeting. Last game, Acuff shot just 5-for-24 and finished with 12 points, but this is a great spot for him to bounce back.
Portland plays at a top-10 pace and ranks 11th in defensive rating. The Kings seem very comfortable handing Acuff the keys to their offense and his incredible 33% usage rate is the highest we've seen from any rookie since [Victor Wembanyama](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003031/Victor+Wembanyama).
**[Franz Wagner](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002654/Franz+Wagner) OVER 6.5 Assists (-127 Novig)**
Wagner is averaging 7.9 assists per game this season, and he's cleared 6.5 assists in seven of nine games since [Paolo Banchero](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002863/Paolo+Banchero) went down with an injury. [Jalen Suggs](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002670/Jalen+Suggs) has a chance to return for this game, but Wagner should continue to facilitate the offense even if he does play.
Wagner finished with just one assist last game, so he'll be looking to bounce back tonight. He gets an elite matchup with Milwaukee to do so. The Bucks have played at the 11th-fastest pace and rank just 29th in defensive rating. They have allowed the fourth-most assists to opponents so far this season.
They have allowed the most assists to point guards this year, so if you want to classify Wagner as the point guard of this offense, which he has been since Paolo Banchero went down, then he gets a big boost in his projection.

## NBA Value Player Props
**[CJ McCollum](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000620/CJ+McCollum) OVER 14.5 Points (-122 DraftKings)**
_Compare at Novig at -122_
McCollum is averaging 13.6 points per game, and he's gone over 14.5 points in two of his last four games since moving to a bench role. He scored 21 points in 24 minutes in his last game against the Lakers, and he gets another elite spot tonight against Chicago.
The Bulls play at the league's fastest pace and rank dead last in defensive efficiency. It is truly the best matchup possible.
[Kingston Flemings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003713/Kingston+Flemings) remains questionable, too, so McCollum could see a boost in minutes if the rookie point guard is sidelined tonight.
**[Bennett Stirtz](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003726/Bennett+Stirtz) OVER 8.5 Points (+102 FanDuel)**
_Compare at Novig at +104_
Stirtz has been a key contributor for Oklahoma City as they deal with multiple injuries. Over his last nine games, Stirtz averaged 10.3 points and played over 23 minutes per game. He fell short of 8.5 points for the first time in six games in his last outing, but he has a great opportunity to bounce back tonight.
The Clippers are playing at a top-10 pace, and they rank just 24th in defensive efficiency. Stirtz should be in line for another 20+ minute role off the bench, and the strong matchup should allow him to clear 8.5 points.
**[Cedric Coward](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003477/Cedric+Coward) OVER 9.5 Rebounds + Assists (-115 Fanatics)**
_Compare at Novig at -117_
Coward is averaging 8.7 rebounds and 2.7 assists per game, giving him 11.4 combined rebounds and assists this season. He has cleared 9.5 in 7 of his last 10 games, including 15 combined against Denver in his most recent game. In the three games he fell short, he finished with exactly nine rebounds and assists.
His rebounding has been particularly impressive, reaching double-digit boards in 5 of his last 10 outings. New Orleans plays at the fourth-fastest pace in the league, so Coward should have a few extra possessions to rack up rebounds and assists. With Coward playing around 28 minutes per game and contributing as a secondary playmaker, he has multiple ways to clear this prop line.
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!
